Log cumulative cost after EVERY call to\\n  WS/results/api_costs.jsonl; hard stop at $8.\\nNEVER let a keyword/substring rule decide any reported number (it is a third\\n  sensitivity layer only).\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- STAGE 5 (PART B)\\n# COVERAGE x STRENGTH WEIGHT FACTORIAL (the decisive test), ~2.5 h GPU\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- \\n# --- 5a. The operator. Reproduce Heretic model.py:abliterate lines 515-619\\n#     EXACTLY, but taking an explicit per-(layer, component) weight so that\\n#     arbitrary coverage SETS are expressible (Heretic's own triangular kernel\\n#     cannot express a stride-2 set).\\ndef abliterate_custom(hm, dEN_all, weights):   # weights: {(layer, component): w}\\n    hm.reset_model()\\n    for (l, comp), w in weights.items():\\n        if w == 0: continue\\n        v = unit(dEN_all[l+1])                      # per-layer direction, +1 shift\\n        for module in hm.get_layer_modules(l)[comp]:\\n            W = dequantize_4bit(module.base_layer.weight).float().view(d_out, -1)\\n            W_org = W; row = norm(W, dim=1, keepdim=True); W = normalize(W, dim=1)\\n            lora_A = (v @ W).view(1,-1); lora_B = (-w * v).view(-1,1)\\n            W = normalize(W + lora_B @ lora_A, dim=1) * row - W_org\\n            torch.manual_seed(SEED); torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)\\n            U,S,Vh = torch.svd_lowrank(W, q=2*3+4, niter=6)   # r = 3\\n            lora_B = U[:,:3] @ diag(sqrt(S[:3])); lora_A = diag(sqrt(S[:3])) @ Vh[:,:3].T\\n            assign into module.lora_A/lora_B[\\\"default\\\"]\\n  UNIT TEST: reproduce the iteration-1 trial-88 edit by feeding Heretic's own\\n  triangular kernel through abliterate_custom and asserting the LoRA factors\\n  match hm.abliterate(...) to <= 1e-5 relative. If it does not match, STOP.\\n  NOTE: d_EN(h) here is the per-layer direction from exp8, i.e. the\\n  direction_scope=\\\"per layer\\\" branch. Orthogonalisation against the ENGLISH\\n  harmless mean is already baked into the stored vectors' construction; assert\\n  and record this in the protocol rather than re-projecting.\\n\\n# --- 5b. Total removal energy, analytic and exact, BEFORE any generation.\\ndef energy(hm):   # from lora_factors(): A [3,d_in], B [d_out,3]\\n    return sum over modules of trace((B^T B) @ (A A^T))     # = ||B A||_F^2, 3x3 work\\n\\n# --- 5c. The design.\\nCOVERAGE_SETS = {\\n  \\\"B1\\\": 1-12, \\\"B2\\\": 13-24, \\\"B3\\\": 25-36, \\\"B4\\\": 37-48,     # contiguous bands\\n  \\\"C24\\\": 1-24, \\\"C36\\\": 1-36,                               # cumulative prefixes\\n  \\\"STR2\\\": every 2nd layer over 1-48, \\\"STR4\\\": every 4th over 1-48,  # strided\\n  \\\"HER88\\\": the support of the iteration-1 selected GaMS3 kernel (trial 88, read\\n           from E1 Optuna journal: per-component max_weight / max_weight_position /\\n           min_weight / min_weight_distance -> the set of layers with w > 0),\\n  \\\"ALL\\\": 1-48 }\\nSTRENGTH c in {0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0}, clipped at Heretic's max_weight bound 1.5\\n  when c would exceed it (record every clip).\\nMATCHED-ENERGY GROUPS: compute E(coverage, c) analytically for the full grid;\\n  then for each of >= 3 target energies E* (chosen to span the grid, e.g. the\\n  25th/50th/75th percentile of the ALL-coverage energies), SOLVE c per coverage\\n  set by bisection on c (E is monotone in c; 6 bisection steps, each just an\\n  adapter build, seconds, no generation) so that |E - E*| <= 10% E*. Each group\\n  must contain at least one NARROW-AND-STRONG cell (a single 12-layer band) and\\n  one BROAD-AND-WEAK cell (STR2 / STR4 / ALL). Record the achieved E for every\\n  cell; a group whose members cannot be matched within 10% is DROPPED and logged.\\nCONTROLS inside every matched-energy group: (0) no-op; (r) layer-matched RANDOM\\n  at the SAME coverage set and the same E, additionally collateral-matched;\\n  (p) energy-matched top harmless-PC direction at the same coverage.\\nANCHOR CELLS (Part F): G1 = single-site d_EN at h*=34 (the EN .57 / SL .50 cell);\\n  CORE = the actual trial-88 LoRA from E1/adapters/gams_selected_path2 (attached,\\n  not rebuilt); SWAP_in = Gemma's trial-96 parameters applied to GaMS3 through\\n  abliterate_custom with GaMS3's own d_EN(h); SWAP_out = GaMS3's trial-88\\n  parameters applied to Gemma (only if the Gemma weights and adapter are present;\\n  otherwise SKIP and say so). Execution order (SCALE GRADUALLY; a timeout must never cost the\\n#     decisive contrast). Write this order into WS/configs/priority.json first.\\n  T0: time 3 cells end to end -> extrapolate -> prune the grid to fit 6 h.\\n  T1: the >= 3 MATCHED-ENERGY GROUPS with their controls   [decisive]\\n  T2: the anchors G1 / CORE / SWAP_in\\n  T3: the remaining coverage cells at c = 1.0             [coverage curve]\\n  T4: the remaining strength levels                        [strength sweep]\\n  T5: SWAP_out, S5X on the two best cells                  [last, optional]\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- STAGE 6 (PART C)\\n# OUTCOMES PER CELL\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- \\nSCREEN  (every cell): S3 half B, 41 JBB harmful pairs x {EN, SL}, 128 new tokens.\\nCONFIRM (T1+T2 cells): S4, harmful + harmless x {EN, SL}, 256 new tokens,\\n  reporting the 70-pair held-out-category 'hoc' stratum SEPARATELY from 'ind'.\\n  (If time is short, confirm on hoc only and say so; rep4 repetition; empty/malformed;\\n  - FLORES dNLL per language (200 S3_flores_dev pairs; chunked log-softmax over\\n    the 262k vocab to avoid OOM);\\n  - harmless 32-token KL vs the unedited model on 100 Dolly continuations;\\n  - utility macro on a frozen 100-item-per-task S7 subsample x 6 tasks x EN/SL,\\n    through the E5 harness replica (T1/T2 cells only).\\nSave EVERY generation, label, per-cell energy, coverage descriptor and cost to\\n  WS/results/cells/<cell>/gens.json and WS/results/panel.parquet.\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- STAGE 7 (PART D)\\n# ANALYSIS -- frozen before the confirmation set was generated\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------- \\nbase  = OLS( SL_residual_refusal ~ log E + EN_judged_refusal + b1 + b3 )\\n  b1 = kernel-weighted per-layer cos(d_EN(h), d_SL(h)) profile (from the npz)\\n  b3 = band kernel masses (fraction of total |w| in each 12-layer band)\\nfull  = base + COVERAGE terms (n covered layers; # gen_plan_experiment_2 — plan for the GaMS3 depth-coverage pod (iteration 3)

```
Planning module. No code was executed and no data was downloaded here; the whole
deliverable is one JSON plan for a downstream experiment executor.

## What this plan covers

The GaMS3-12B-Instruct half of iteration 3's C1/C2 test:

- **Part A** — a DEV-only, activation-only **per-language depth-redundancy index**
  (`index_EN`, `index_SL`) from cumulative-prefix / cumulative-suffix /
  leave-one-band-out ablation of the frozen per-layer English refusal directions,
  frozen and hashed before any confirmation data is generated.
- **Part B** — a **coverage × strength weight-edit factorial** built through
  Heretic's own operator, with ≥3 matched-total-removal-energy groups, each
  holding a narrow-and-strong and a broad-and-weak cell, plus no-op,
  energy-and-collateral-matched random, and energy-matched-PC controls.
- **Parts C–D** — partial-aware four-way judged outcomes (refused / PARTIAL /
  complied / invalid) with RefusEU guard ASR, over-refusal, GlotLID, FLORES and
  utility, analysed against predictions frozen in advance.
- **Parts F–G** — GaMS3 anchors (single-site d_EN, core trial 88, both swap
  cells) and the cross-model contrast table against the sibling Gemma pod.

The GaMS3-specific prediction is the **equivalence** `index_SL ≈ index_EN`
(±8 layers), against the Gemma pod's `index_SL > index_EN`.

## Layout
```

```
Plan written to `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`, with `README.md` and an empty `.aii/manifest.yaml` (this module produced no heavy paths).

**What the plan says, in short**

The GaMS3-12B-Instruct pod of iteration 3's depth-coverage test, on one A4500 (`gpu_basic`), inside 6 h and ≤$8 of OpenRouter spend (expected ~$2.5, possibly $0).

- **Part A** — activation-only DEV redundancy index from cumulative prefixes, cumulative suffixes and leave-one-band-out ablation of the frozen per-layer `d_EN(h)`; `index_L` = minimal cumulative layer count taking judged harmful refusal below 0.5; frozen and hashed with `frozen_predictions.json` **before** any confirmation generation.
- **Part B** — the contrast iteration 2 could not make: coverage × strength crossed, with total removal energy computed *analytically* from the LoRA factors before anything runs, and `c` solved per coverage set by bisection so ≥3 matched-energy groups each hold a narrow-and-strong and a broad-and-weak cell, plus no-op, energy-**and**-collateral-matched random, and energy-matched-PC controls.
- **Parts C–G** — partial-aware four-way judged outcomes, guard ASR, over-refusal, GlotLID, FLORES, utility; the pre-frozen analysis; GaMS3 anchors including both swap cells; the cross-model table.

**Three things I made sharper than the direction stated.** The GaMS3 prediction is an *equivalence*, so it now carries a pre-declared ±8-layer margin rather than being scored as a null. Heretic's triangular kernel cannot express a stride-2 set, so the plan specifies an `abliterate_custom` that reproduces `model.py:515-619` verbatim with an explicit per-layer weight, gated by a unit test against `hm.abliterate` — if that gate fails, the fallback drops the strided arms and says coverage is confounded with contiguity. And GaMS3's single-site `d_EN` already reaches SL .50, so `index_SL` may sit at the floor with no headroom to measure; the plan treats that as the predicted cross-model contrast to report, not a failure to hide.

The falsifier is pre-declared: if coverage adds dR² < 0.05 and broad-and-weak does not beat narrow-and-strong at matched energy, the artifact reports "Slovene simply needs more total edit" with the same care as a positive.
```
